   122	    const s0 = initLoopState(SPEC(), io());
   123	    let s = s0;
   124	    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i += 1) s = applyLoopEvent(s, { type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: 'gate-2-a', verdict: 'BLOCKED' });

   171	  it('smoke-await enters awaiting-smoke-approval; a VERIFIED smoke-approve records the token and returns to running', () => {
   172	    const { initLoopState, applyLoopEvent, LOOP_EVENT } = M();
   173	    const s0 = initLoopState(SPEC(), io());
   174	    const awaiting = applyLoopEvent(s0, { type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE, token: 'x', verified: true });
   175	    // Guard: an approve while NOT awaiting is refused (state unchanged).
   176	    assert.equal(awaiting.status, 'running', 'approve while running is a no-op (still running)');
   177	    assert.ok(!awaiting.smokeApproval, 'no approval recorded when not awaiting');

   188	  it('a STALE/unverified smoke-approve does NOT return to running (guarded transition — finding 2)', () => {
   189	    const { initLoopState, applyLoopEvent, LOOP_EVENT } = M();
   190	    const s0 = initLoopState(SPEC(), io());

   192	    // verified:false is the caller's verifySmokeToken having found drift.

   198	  it('a smoke-approve with NO verified field is NOT an approval — missing != approved (iter-2 finding 1)', () => {
   199	    const { initLoopState, applyLoopEvent, LOOP_EVENT } = M();
   200	    const s0 = initLoopState(SPEC(), io());
   201	    // Outside the awaiting state it is a no-op (consistent with the guard pin above):

   206	
   207	    // While awaiting, a missing verified field is treated as unverified (not approved):
   208	    // it must NOT resume, mirroring the stale-token expectations.
   209	    const inAwait = applyLoopEvent(applyLoopEvent(s0, { type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT }), { type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE, token: 'tok' });

   211	    assert.ok(['awaiting-smoke-approval', 'parked'].includes(inAwait.status), `unverified approval refuses or parks; got ${inAwait.status}`);
   212	    assert.ok(!inAwait.smokeApproval, 'no accepted approval is recorded without an explicit verified:true');
   213	  });

   101	  it('a token verifies ok against its own inputs', () => {
   102	    const { smokeApprovalToken, verifySmokeToken } = M();
   103	    const token = smokeApprovalToken(INPUTS());
   104	    const r = verifySmokeToken(token, INPUTS());
   105	    assert.equal(r.ok, true, 'unchanged inputs re-derive the same token');
   106	  });
   107	
   108	  it('a single drifted input => ok:false naming exactly that input', () => {
   109	    const { smokeApprovalToken, verifySmokeToken } = M();
   110	    const token = smokeApprovalToken(INPUTS());
   111	    for (const [field, needle] of Object.entries(FIELD_NEEDLE)) {
   112	      const drifted = { ...INPUTS(), [field]: `${INPUTS()[field]}-MOVED` };
   113	      const r = verifySmokeToken(token, drifted);
   114	      assert.equal(r.ok, false, `${field} drift must fail verification`);
   115	      assert.ok(Array.isArray(r.driftedInputs) && r.driftedInputs.length > 0, 'driftedInputs is a non-empty array');
   116	      assert.ok(r.driftedInputs.some((/** @type {string} */ n) => needle.test(n)), `driftedInputs names ${field}; got ${JSON.stringify(r.driftedInputs)}`);
   117	    }
   118	  });
   119	
   120	  it('multiple drifted inputs are ALL named', () => {
   121	    const { smokeApprovalToken, verifySmokeToken } = M();
   122	    const token = smokeApprovalToken(INPUTS());
   123	    const drifted = { ...INPUTS(), gitHead: 'moved-head', childAssignment: 'implementer=claude-code/claude-fable-5' };
   124	    const r = verifySmokeToken(token, drifted);
   125	    assert.equal(r.ok, false);
   126	    assert.ok(r.driftedInputs.some((/** @type {string} */ n) => /head/i.test(n)), 'git head drift named');
   127	    assert.ok(r.driftedInputs.some((/** @type {string} */ n) => /child|assignment/i.test(n)), 'child assignment drift named');
   128	  });
